# Joshua White playlists: EGW quotation check

Checked 2026-10-13 against the local corpus with `bible egw lookup` and `bible egw search`. The index is lexical FTS; the embedding model is not on this laptop. Each quotation he reads or closely paraphrases has one row; bare allusions are listed separately.

**Statuses**

- **VERIFIED:** the wording matches, apart from caption noise or trivial omissions. A context flag is added where his use is wider than the source.
- **DIFFERS:** a misquote, a splice of two or more paragraphs, added words, or a wrong reference. The real text or ref is given.
- **NOT FOUND:** the searches tried are listed, with the nearest hit.

**How rows are counted.** Rows are distinct within each check batch below. A paragraph he reads again in another playlist counts again there; repeats inside a batch are marked "dup" and not counted. The two non-White speakers in playlist C are counted separately.

| Playlist                                | Rows    | VERIFIED | DIFFERS | NOT FOUND |
| --------------------------------------- | ------- | -------- | ------- | --------- |
| A. Bogenhofen 2026 (A1–A8)              | 172     | 165      | 5       | 2         |
| B. Autumn Leaves NZ (B1–B8)             | 124     | 113      | 10      | 1         |
| C. Trinidad (13 White talks)            | 164     | 154      | 6       | 4         |
| D. True Education 3 Part Series (D1–D3) | 95      | 94       | 1       | 0         |
| E. True Education (E1–E8)               | 96      | 91       | 4       | 1         |
| F. Uchee Pines seminars (F1–F7)         | 70      | 68       | 1       | 1         |
| **Joshua White, all**                   | **721** | **685**  | **27**  | **9**     |
| C3 and C7 (other speakers, kept apart)  | 15      | 15       | 0       | 0         |

**Corrected when D–F were merged** (2026-10-13; each row carries its note):

- C1 1:17:39 "the highest attainments … doing others good" is CT 505.3 (VERIFIED, was NOT FOUND).
- C1 1:02:10, the Eden "divine system of teaching" passage, is Sutherland's _Living Fountains or Broken Cisterns_ (1900), named by him in D1. It is no longer counted as EGW.
- C1 0:42:50 "the very foundation of true education is in the fear of the Lord" compresses two sentences of 13LtMs Ms 25, 1898 par. 23 (DIFFERS, was NOT FOUND). CT 505.2 is the citable form.
- C8 0:56:11 "record your name beside David Livingstone, Hudson Taylor" embellishes AH 235.1 (DIFFERS, was NOT FOUND).
- C13 0:52:55 is Ed 77.2, not MH 400.1 (still VERIFIED).
- B2's "Babylon was the educational center of the world" is Haskell, SDP 29.1.

One row was corrected in the first merge. B6 0:38:29 "not only Adam's dwelling, but his schoolroom" was NOT FOUND in its batch because the corpus spells it "school-room". It is GCDB March 6, 1899 par. 36, as at A1 0:44:15 and C1 1:12:57.

**Attribution caution.** That GCDB 1899 paragraph is filed as EGW in the corpus, but its reprint in PH140 7.3 is signed "—P.C." Don't use it until its source is confirmed. Ed 20.1 carries the same idea safely.

## What goes wrong

His EGW is more accurate than Ritsema's across all six playlists: 95% VERIFIED (685 of 721) against 93%, and DIFFERS on under 4% of rows against 5%. The 2016–2018 talks (D, F) are the most accurate. The faults fall into four kinds:

- **Splices** read as one quotation. Never take a reference from his slides; use the corpus ref here.
  - Ed 41.1 + 187.1
  - 4T 498.2 + 498.3
  - PK 612.2 + 614.1
  - PK 587.2 + 591.2
  - CD 57.4 + 55.3
  - CG 304.3 + 305.1 + 315.1
  - Ed 51.3 + 52.4
  - GW92 388.1 + 1SM 172.2 + 1SM 401.2
  - 1SM 68.1 + 68.3
  - Ed 46.2 + FE 96.2 (D2)
  - Ed 106.3 + 107.2 (E2)
  - AH 32.2 + RH Feb 21, 1893 par. 8 (F5)
  - the "danger signal" splice of 18LtMs Ms 155 (1903) and 21LtMs Lt 70 (1906)
- **Context cut short.** The quotations are verified, but they are used against what follows them:
  - **CT 205.1** goes on to "send them to schools … Scriptural foundation".
  - **Ms 100, 1902** goes on to the church school plus "Teach the Scriptures to your children yourselves".
  - **The "danger signal"** is about the Battle Creek _Sanitarium_, not a church school.
  - **AH 215.1** ("house-band") sits under "Exercise authority with humility".
  - **AH 90.2** was written to a young woman preparing for marriage.
  - **MH 402.2** "use" means imparting knowledge to others.
  - **CL 9.4** says remaining in cities "unnecessarily".
  - **FE 21.2** is from 1872 (= 3T 137.2), not "the late 1880s".
- **Wording changed:**
  - MYP 242.2 drops "resisting temptation".
  - Ed 296.1 drops "conquered".
  - FE 156.1 drops "may".
  - AH 466.2 is reconstructed.
  - "Let us learn together" is CT 436.1 "Let us climb together", not COL.
  - GCDB 1899 has "in the education of our children" added and is presented as the 1872 message.
  - CG 300.3 is read as infancy "through seven"; it says "six or seven" (E2).
  - "The very foundation of true education is in the fear of the Lord" compresses Ms 25, 1898 (C1, E1).
  - "Record your name beside David Livingstone" embellishes AH 235.1 (C8).
- **His own lines presented as quotations** (the NOT FOUND rows):
  - "truth not lived is not truth" (nearest ChS 97.4);
  - "not a shred of human devising";
  - "most influential position attainable … the world could be without kings";
  - students "should not go into debt" (nearest CT 527.1, a preference);
  - the mother/father/sister "balancing" line (nearest AH 35.5);
  - "all sin had its root in the exaltation of human wisdom above faith in the divine" (nearest Ed 25.2);
  - "recognize his own weakness and insufficiency" (in the middle of Ed 126.2);
  - (the long Eden "divine system of teaching" passage, once listed here, is Sutherland's _Living Fountains_: he names it in D1.)

**Non-EGW material** read without a name: Sutherland's _Living Fountains_ (C1; named in D1); John White's "Parable of the Orange Trees", retold as apples (D3, F4, F7); Haskell about ten times in E (listed in its batch). Earlier: Haskell's _Story of Daniel the Prophet_ (SDP 49.1, 163.2 and others). It is verified in the corpus where its OCR is clean. "Jerusalem was destroyed because the education of her children was neglected" matches the Babylonian Talmud, Shabbat 119b ("Jerusalem was destroyed only because they neglected the schoolchildren"), which Haskell gives as a "Hebrew maxim". Check the wording in a printed edition before relying on it. Each batch's own non-EGW table follows its EGW table.

The batch tables follow, as checked.

- **VERIFIED**: wording matches apart from caption noise or trivial omissions.

**Counts (quotations):** VERIFIED 90 · DIFFERS 0 · NOT FOUND 1

(A1 43 · A2 10 · A3 12, including the NOT FOUND · A4 26. Repeats in A2/A4 are counted once and marked "dup".)

Nothing reached DIFFERS. He reads from slides, and the wording is close throughout. The weak spots are **context**, all flagged in the Note column:

- MH 402.2's "use" means imparting knowledge;
- AH 215.1's humility frame is dropped;
- AH 90.2 was written to a young woman;
- AH 293.1 is about authority;
- FLB 242.5 is about performers;
- FE 95.5 is stretched to the prophets of today;
- the GCDB 1899 "Adam's schoolroom" attribution is uncertain;
- the "eight or ten" line is split by sex as his own gloss.

VERIFIED 49 · DIFFERS 5 · NOT FOUND 3

### Non-EGW quotations

#### Haskell, _The Story of Daniel the Prophet_ (1901)

All of these readings were verified in the corpus (pioneer scope):

- **Hephzibah or Mary:** SDP 16.1, 16.3 (B2 0:32:51; C10).
- **Daniel's godly mother:** SDP 19.3 + 19.4 (B2 0:49:13; C10).
  - SDP 19.3 dates the captivity to "607 B.C." with Daniel "not over eighteen". This conflicts with White's own 605 BC and age-15 arithmetic.
- **"Now can be seen the results of the home training… children of the home school":** SDP 20.1, 20.2 (B2 0:50:58; C10).
- **"Where are the parents":** SDP 26.1 (B2 0:53:11; C10).
- **SDP 26.2** (C10 1:16:18). He reads only its last sentences. The omitted middle says youth "rooted and grounded in the Scriptures" could "enter worldly universities… and come forth unsullied", and cites the Waldenses.
- **"From what homes will come the Daniels":** SDP 49.1 (B2 0:56:01; C10 1:20:14). Read without attribution.
- **Plato:** SDP 183.2 (C13 0:20:05). SDP 183.3 adds that Plato's teaching was "often good".
- **"Greece captured their minds":** SDP 184.1 (C13 0:16:36).
- **Alexandria, "in the days of Ptolemy Soter":** SDP 190.1 (C13 0:24:43).
- **"Not a family in Judah to whom the education of a prophet could be intrusted":** SDP 190.2 (C13 0:33:06).
- **The nations should have sent their youth to Jerusalem:** SDP 196.1–197.1 (C13 0:31:27). In C13 he presents it as the continuation of the Gilbert article; it is Haskell. B4 attributes it correctly.
- **Greek learning still rules:** SDP 197.3 (C13 0:36:28).
- **"Choose your shrine":** SDP 198.1 (C13 0:50:38). He leaves out Haskell's creation and age-of-the-earth context.
- **The Hebrew maxim that Jerusalem was destroyed because the education of her children was neglected:** SDP 14.3 (B8 0:09:51).
- **"Trained in the fear of the Lord":** SDP 36.3 (B8 0:12:04).
- **Saddest commentary:** SDP 162.2/163.1 (B8 0:40:28).
- **"Jewish homes should have been schools":** SDP 163.2 (B8 0:37:50).
- **Schools of the prophets in Babylon; "Did Israel do so?":** SDP 163.3 (B8 0:16:33, 0:20:20).
- **Cyrus was ready:** SDP 164.1 (B8 0:27:33).
- **Gabriel for 36 years:** SDP 168.1 (B8 0:28:41).
- **"The same promises":** SDP 170.1 (B8 0:29:14).
- **Persecution:** SDP 173.1 (B8 0:31:10).
- **Modern Babylon:** SDP 38.1 (B8 0:49:36).

#### Other authors and sources

- **E. A. Sutherland, _Living Fountains or Broken Cisterns_ (1900)** (C13 0:14:15). Not in the corpus (pioneer or all scope). Needs the book.
- **F. C. Gilbert, _The Ministry_ (1933)** (C13 0:26:24–0:31:27). Not in the corpus. Needs the article. Errors as spoken:
  - "rabbi was a Greek degree" is wrong; rabbi is Hebrew/Aramaic.
  - "the Sanhedrin which crucified Jesus" is wrong; Rome executed, the Sanhedrin condemned.
  - The Avot 5:21 age scheme (Scripture at 5, Mishnah at 10, Gemara at 15) is real; the quote leaves out its later stages.
- **Plato, "science of sciences… dialectic"** (C13 0:21:52). The idea is the dialectic as the capstone in Republic VII; the wording is not Plato's. Needs a primary source.
- **American Humanist Association / Humanists International definitions** (C13 0:37:45). They match the organizations' public wording but are outside the corpus. Checkable.
- **John Dewey, "Faith in the prayer-hearing God is an unproved and outmoded faith. There is no God and there is no soul…"** (C13 0:40:13). Not traceable to Dewey's works; widely circulated as a misattribution. Do not use.
- **The "Ellen White and Maria Montessori" book** (B4 0:31:47; C13). No author or publisher given. Needs a primary source.
- **Charlotte Mason as a "religious humanist" who taught "inherent good in the child"** (C13 0:42:39). Contradicted by her first principle: children are "born persons… not born either good or bad". She was a practising Anglican.
- **Vodie Baucham, "send our children to Caesar… come home as Romans"** (B8 0:55:11). Widely attributed. Needs the sermon source.
- **Statistics in B8:**
  - "less than 5% learned from teachers" (0:55:40);
  - "14,000 hours" (0:56:01);
  - "3.5 minutes a week of meaningful conversation" (0:56:01).

  He names no source for any of them. They are leads only; the last is a dated 1990s figure.

- **Merriam-Webster, "admonition"** (B8 0:01:52). Matches the dictionary sense.
- **Tyndale's "boy that driveth the plough"** (C11 0:05:30). In the corpus at GC 246.3 and Foxe (FBOM 115.9), so the retort is verified. Errors as spoken:
  - "head chopped off" is wrong; he was strangled and burned in 1536.
  - "translated the majority of the KJV" is overstated (the NT, largely).
- **Paper-versus-screen reading research** (C11 0:08:16). No study named. Leads: Delgado et al., _Educational Research Review_ 2018 (meta-analysis, a paper advantage for informational text); Ackerman & Goldsmith 2011 (overconfidence on screen). His claim that "the parietal cortex is emotional processing" is wrong.
- **The "missing day" / astronomers tracing the sun going back** (C10 0:51:36). A legend with no source. Do not use.

**Counts:** VERIFIED 69 · DIFFERS 2 · NOT FOUND 1 (after the D and E merges: 1:17:39 found as CT 505.3; 1:02:10 re-classed as Sutherland, not counted; 0:42:50 found as a compression, DIFFERS)

### Non-EGW quotations and named sources (what could be checked)

Local checks used `bible egw search --scope all`. Nothing below is in the local corpora, so each item is a lead to check in the named source.

- **E. A. Sutherland, _Studies in Christian Education_** (C1 1:18:13; C2 0:52:35): "the memory was cultivated as a means of keeping down…", "showy results", "content to repeat set lessons", "endless treadmill". Not in the corpus (`--scope all`, four phrasings). The book is named, so it can be checked in print. Denominational author.
- **Raymond Moore** (C1 1:03:51): early-childhood goal of "a sense of their own worth". Not in the corpus (`--scope all`). No book named.
- **Jefferson** (C1): agriculture-decay line. No source; likely misattributed.
- **Jesuit maxim** (C1): "until the age of seven… ours forever". Traditional; no primary source.
- **Prince of Wales ordinances, 1473** (C1): real history (Edward IV for the future Edward V at Ludlow). The details need the ordinance text.
- **Carter G. Woodson, _The Mis-Education of the Negro_ (1933)** (C2): "control a man's thinking". A real book; checkable.
- **Hitler, "fortunate for leaders that men do not think"** (C2): no primary source; likely spurious. His claim that Hitler was "voted in by a majority" is also wrong.
- **Fichte, _Addresses to the German Nation_ (1807); Russell, _The Impact of Science on Society_ (1952); Cubberley (1934)** (C2): real and checkable. The same chain as Ritsema's.
- **"Rockefeller General Education Board, 1913"** (C2): the words are F. T. Gates, GEB _Occasional Papers_ No. 1.
- **W. T. Harris, "99 out of 100… automata"** (C2): usually cited via Gatto from _The Philosophy of Education_ (1906). Needs the primary page.
- **"The child is my child" attributed to Dewey** (C2): he flags the author as uncertain himself. No source; do not use.
- **Brain/media sources (C4, C5): no primary study checked here.** Victoria Dunckley; Brad Huddleston, _Digital Cocaine_; Cris Rowan; Common Sense Media's 173-study review (2008); Loh & Kanai, _PLoS ONE_ 2014 (title read aloud; correlational); _NYT_ "In Classroom of Future, Stagnant Scores" (Richtel, Sept 2011: Kyrene, Schauer, Vander Ark, Cuban); Dynarski et al. 2007 (16 products, not 15); _NYT_ Waldorf School of the Peninsula (Oct 2011, Alan Eagle); _NYT_ Bilton (Sept 2014, Jobs, Chris Anderson); Manfred Spitzer; Carla Hannaford, _Smart Moves_. The named _NYT_, _PLoS ONE_ and Dynarski items can be checked. The claims "94% Ivy League" for the Waldorf school, permanent retina damage from blue light, blood pressure of 180/140 in game testing and "100% of mass shooters are gamers" look wrong and need a primary source before any use.
- **Elliott Barker** (C6): "over 90%" of psychopaths were disturbed in the first three years. He hedges ("if I remember right"); needs a source.
- **_Child Development_ article** (C6): parent–child relationship quality moderates moral discourse. Likely Laible & Thompson 2000; check before use.
- **"Jerusalem was destroyed because the education of her children was neglected"** (C6 0:57:39): not in the EGW corpus. It is a Talmudic saying (b. Shabbat 119b).

## Joshua White, _Trinidad True Education Seminar_ (playlist C, batch 6): EGW quotation check

Checked 2026-10-12 against the local corpus with `bible egw search` (lexical FTS; no embedding model on this machine) and `bible egw lookup`. Videos: C8 mn6M9ID_bLU, C9 pAjI6IkO66k, C14 QqRhQfMn1Xw, C15 WH3QrmDHQlg. Other speakers (C3 B5rEw1e1Z5M, C7 shBd2jDoNz4) are in a separate sub-table and are not counted with White.

- **VERIFIED**: wording matches apart from caption noise or trivial omissions.

**Counts (White quotations):** VERIFIED 61 · DIFFERS 4 · NOT FOUND 1 (after the F merge: C8 0:56:11 placed as an embellishment of AH 235.1)

(C8 22: 20 V, 1 D, 1 NF · C9 22: all V · C14 19: 16 V, 2 D, 1 NF · C15 3: all V. Repeats are counted once and marked "dup".)

Weak spots:

- **Dating**: FE 21.2 is put in "the late 1880s" to align it with secular "mind-control" quotes. It is from 1872.
- **Splices**: the C14 0:19:49 slide and Ed 51.3 + 52.4.
- **Context widening**: CG 342.2, CG 521.4, CSW 57.1, Ev 525.1, LS 132.1, AH 115.2.
- **Two "quotes" that are not EGW**: Livingstone/Hudson Taylor, and the debt "rule".

Repeats inside this batch, not counted: D2 0:15:49 "the true object of education is to restore the image of God in the soul" (CE 63.3 = PP 595.2, D1 0:07:48); D2 0:16:19 Ed 30.2; D3 0:13:31–0:14:35 Ed 15.1–15.2 (D2); D3 0:18:16 Ed 13.1 (D1).

**Counts:** VERIFIED 94 · DIFFERS 1 · NOT FOUND 0

### Paraphrases and mentions (not counted)

- D2 0:00:00 "the book Acts of the Apostles … the reason we don't have more of the outpouring of the Holy Spirit is because we don't expect and ask for more". A loose allusion; nearest AA 50.2 ("why do we not hunger and thirst for the gift of the Spirit? … pray for it").
- D3 0:33:27 "this quote just a paraphrase: if Adventists had held fast their faith, push[ed] forward to spread the messages of the third angel, we would have gone home by now". 1SM 68.1 (= Ev 695–696). Fair in substance; drops "after the great disappointment in 1844". Denominational.
- D1 0:08:19 "True education needs to fit us for earthly life, but it must also fit us for heavenly life": his own summary line (cf. CE 24.1).
- D1 0:36:30 "Go to book Education page 267 … three ways … to know and understand the will of God": a pointer to Ed 267.3, not read.

### Corrections to earlier batches (for the merge)

- **C1 1:17:39** "the Christian … aims to reach the highest attainments … for the purpose of doing others good" (NOT FOUND, nearest 5T 104.1) is **CT 505.3** ("The Christian aims to reach the highest attainments for the purpose of doing others good"), with the fuller form in **CE 51.1** (= FE 119.1). It should be re-marked VERIFIED. The "What goes wrong" bullet listing it among "his own lines presented as quotations" should go.
- **C1 1:02:10** Eden "God himself visited them … the way of immortality … according to the divine system of teaching" (NOT FOUND, "perhaps Sutherland"). In D1 0:24:33 he introduces the same passage as "from the incredible book by E. A. Sutherland, _Living Fountains or Broken Cisterns_" (as at A1 0:43:07). It is Sutherland, not EGW: re-class it as a non-EGW quotation read unnamed in C1, not as NOT FOUND EGW.

### Non-EGW quotations

- **E. A. Sutherland, _Living Fountains or Broken Cisterns_ (1900)** (D1 0:24:33): "God planted a garden eastward in Eden, and from the beauties of the earth chose the most beautiful spot … divine system of teaching". Named on screen. Not in the local corpus; check the 1900 printing. It settles C1 1:02:10.
- **Japan 1938, Dr. Mizuno** (D1 0:00:00 and 0:43:18): "Dr. Toshio Yamagata and Elder Francis Millard of the Japan Union Mission". The quotation "If you follow your plan, you have no reason to worry; if you do not follow your plan, you have no reason to exist" needs a primary source (denominational lore; the same story closes B6 and C1, here with names).
- **Hippocampus study** (D1 0:16:43): "higher fit children showed greater bilateral hippocampal volumes and superior relational memory task performance". This matches Chaddock et al., "A neuroimaging investigation of the association between aerobic fitness, hippocampal volume, and memory performance in preadolescent children", _Brain Research_ 1358 (2010). Checkable; correlational.
- **John Ratey, _Spark_ (2008)** (D1 0:17:49): "an unparalleled stimulus, creating an environment in which the brain is ready, willing, and able to learn". Unnamed here; named in B6.
- **"Proprioceptive system"** makes physical activity a precondition for early academics (D1 0:17:49). Needs a primary source; don't speak.
- **Thomas Jefferson**, "No nation will long survive the decay of its agriculture" (D1 0:24:03). No known Jefferson source; likely misattributed (as at C1).
- **Gardening outcomes** (D1 0:26:05): self-esteem, patience, test scores, and one experimental group outscoring controls in "general information, reading recognition, reading comprehension, total reading, mathematics, spelling, and written language". No study named; needs a primary source.
- **_Mycobacterium vaccae_** (D1 0:27:43–0:31:20): "Dirt is the new Prozac" is the title of a 2007 _Discover_ article (Glausiusz); the mice "twice as fast, half the anxiety" result is Matthews & Jenks (2010 ASM meeting; _Behavioural Processes_ 2013). The Chris Lowry "University of Bristol" quotation fits the 2007 Bristol press release for Lowry et al., _Neuroscience_ 2007. His benefit list (fights cancer, treats arthritis, "make you smarter") and the hand-soaking cure overreach. Leads only.
- **_New England Journal of Medicine_ farm study** (D1 0:30:18): "greater diversity of microbial exposure among children who live on farms … protection from the development of asthma". Fits Ege et al., NEJM 364 (2011): 701–709. "Less than half the incidence of allergies and asthma" is his gloss.
- **Tanzania statistics** (D1 0:43:49–0:45:53): 2001 primary education program; 2008–2012 UN attendance 92–96%; secondary 32% of boys and 24% of girls; 71% of the population under 30; UNFPA one in six girls aged 15–19 pregnant each year; 5–8,000 pregnancy expulsions a year 2008–2011 (cf. the Center for Reproductive Rights' _Forced Out_, 2013, which gives over 55,000 in a decade); about a million child domestic workers. Each needs its primary report.
- **Kibidula mission and Reach Switzerland, 2004** (D1 0:46:25): about 60 students on three farm units, self-sufficient, about two hours a week of academics, national exams passed, "99% … baptized". Needs a primary source (institutional report).
- **Luther and Melanchthon "transformed the nation of Germany in one generation"** (D1 0:52:12): the Sutherland (_SICE_) claim as in B7; needs a primary source.
- **John White (author), "The Parable of the Orange Trees"** (D3 0:02:03–0:11:57): retold uncredited in the first person with apples ("Apple Pickers' Manual", "leaving the neglected country, entering the home country"). The original has orange groves, "Leaving NEGLECTED COUNTY—Entering HOME COUNTY", and an "Orange Picker's Manual". A circulating PDF exists; the book source needs confirming.
- **Thomas Edison's "ten thousand" attempts** (D3 0:22:00): popular lore; no primary source.
- **David Livingstone** (D3 0:22:32): aims, hardships and Westminster Abbey burial (1874) are correct.
- **World-suffering statistics** (D3 0:29:17–0:30:19): about 30 million enslaved (Global Slavery Index 2013: 29.8 million); 19,000 under-five deaths a day in 2011 (UNICEF 2012); over a billion children severely deprived (UNICEF, _State of the World's Children_ 2005); one in nine undernourished (FAO 2014–15); a billion children in conflict-affected countries (UNICEF 2009). "About 20 million orphaned children" doesn't match UNICEF (about 140 million single and double orphans; about 15 million double). "22 million child refugees" and "over 50% … domestic violence" need sources.
- **E. M. Bounds, _Power Through Prayer_ (1907), ch. 1** (D3 0:50:17): "men are God's method … He does not anoint plans, but men". Real; checkable (as at B3).
- **John Wesley** (D3 0:51:23): "give me one hundred men … who love only God … hate only sin … shake the gates of hell and bring in the kingdom of God in one generation". The real letter (to Alexander Mather, 6 Aug 1777) reads "Give me one hundred preachers who fear nothing but sin, and desire nothing but God … such alone will shake the gates of hell and set up the kingdom of heaven upon earth". The "one generation" is not in it.
- **Kenya orphanage founder, 283 children** (D3 0:51:54): second-hand from a California camp meeting; unnamed. Needs a primary source.

---

## Joshua White, True Education playlist (E1–E8): EGW quotation check

Checked 2026-10-13 against the local corpus with `bible egw lookup` and `bible egw search` (lexical FTS only; the embedding model is not on this laptop). Each quotation he reads or closely paraphrases has one row. Repeats inside this batch (E1–E8) are marked "dup" and not counted. The batch is checked in video order, so a quotation counts where it first appears; E1, E5 and E6 are one core talk, so most of E5 and E6 are dups of E1. ### Paraphrases and mentions (not counted)

- **E1 0:44:14** "made a part of the everyday curriculum to work for the needs of those in the community": his gloss on CT 545.3.
- **E5 0:40:02** "sending them forth to distant lands": CT 549.1.
- **E5 0:42:35, E6 0:11:29 and 1:05:28** "we're told… it was a result of false education that the Jewish nation rejected Jesus". The nearest is DA 299.3 ("to undo the work that had been wrought by false education"); his causal claim is wider.
- **E5 0:21:50** "the sources of truth… the Bible, the Spirit of Prophecy and nature": his gloss on Ed 17.2's "nature and revelation".
- **E2 0:45:59** "parents should play with their children" (≈ AH 192.2).
- **E3 0:11:21** "stand individually and alone": his line (nearest Mar 217.5).
- **E3 0:27:26** "time divided": AH 508.3 (back-reference).

### Corrections to earlier batches (for the merge)

- **C1 1:17:39:** "the Christian… aims to reach the highest attainments… doing others good" is **CT 505.3**, VERIFIED. Remove it from the NOT FOUND list and from "His own lines presented as quotations".
- **C1 0:42:50:** the "very foundation of true education is in the fear of the Lord" line resolves to a compression of 13LtMs Ms 25, 1898 par. 23 (cf. CT 505.2). Move it from NOT FOUND to DIFFERS.
- **C13 0:52:55:** read Ed 77.2, not MH 400.1.
- **B2:** "Babylon… educational center of the world" is Haskell, SDP 29.1.
- **notes.md C4:** the outdoor-violin myopia child is a girl.

### Non-EGW quotations

- **E. A. Sutherland, _Studies in Christian Education_** (E1 0:07:22, 0:40:07, 0:41:40; E5; E6):
  - "the world was approaching the great day of atonement… impossible for them to acceptably carry the closing message unless… true science of education";
  - the Jesuit memory passage (Painter, via Sutherland);
  - the "papal… endless treadmill".

  None is in the corpus (`--scope all`). He says the first "is quoting the Spirit of Prophecy", but only "true science of education" (1MCP 53.2) matches. In E6 he names Sutherland as a founder of the Madison school (denominational).

- **Sutherland, _Living Fountains_** (E7): as at C13.
- **Japan, 1938** (E1, E6): Dr Mizuno, Toshio Yamagata, Francis Millard (Japan Union Mission). Adventist lore; needs a primary source (an Adventist periodical).
- **Endorsements of _Education_** (E1, E6): Mysore 1947, Ghana's education secretary, John Michaelis (Berkeley), Florence Stratemeyer (Columbia; "50 years ahead"; "Roman Catholic"). These circulate in Adventist sources. Need primary sources; don't use.
- **"Over 60 schools"** in the Second Great Awakening (E1, E5): Sutherland, unsourced.
- **Edward, Prince of Wales** (E6): born 2 Nov 1470 (correct); his father's 1473 ordinances for the household at Ludlow are real. He reads "from an article"; check the ordinance text before use (as no. 90 already says).
- **Physical-activity benefits** (E1, E5 "JAMA Pediatrics", E6 "American Medical Association of Pediatrics"): needs the paper.
  - "Higher fit children… greater bilateral hippocampal volumes" = Chaddock et al., _Brain Research_ 2010 (real).
  - "Ready, willing and able to learn" = John Ratey, _Spark_ (2008).
- **Gardening:**
  - confidence, patience, science, "test scores", "IQ" (E1, E5, E6): needs sources; IQ is doubtful;
  - E6's "experimental group outperformed… general information, reading recognition… spelling, written language": the subtests look like a standard achievement battery. Needs the study.
- **_Mycobacterium vaccae_** (E1, E6):
  - Lowry et al. 2007 (mice); "Dirt: the new Prozac" (_Discover_, 2007); a mouse maze study behind "smarter";
  - "fights cancer, treats arthritis, raises IQ" is overstated;
  - farm children's lower allergy and asthma (Riedler 2001; von Mutius) is real, but tied to broad microbial exposure;
  - the elderly-depression "cure" is a secondhand anecdote.
- **The "false education" quotations:**

  | Source as spoken                                                                  | Status                                                                                                                                    |
  | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
  | Cubberley, _Changing Conceptions of Education_ (1909, E5, E3)                     | Real book; check the exact sentence. "The child is my child" is given to Cubberley here and to Dewey in C2, so the attribution conflicts. |
  | "Rockefeller General Education Board, 1913"                                       | F. T. Gates, _Occasional Papers_ No. 1                                                                                                    |
  | Edward A. Ross, _Social Control_ ("plastic lumps of human dough… kneading board") | Real; 1901, not 1900                                                                                                                      |
  | "John Dewey" ("less than an hour a day… broken in about a generation")            | No known source; probably spurious                                                                                                        |
  | Horace Mann ("parents as giving hostages to our cause")                           | Needs a primary source                                                                                                                    |

  Same frame as Ritsema's; the series leaves it out.

- **Romania** (E4): a village friend said communism "wasn't that bad… most… happened in the cities". A personal anecdote, used as evidence for leaving cities.
- **Ezra 2:64–65** (E4): 42,360 + 7,337 + 200 = 49,897. His "49,997, three short of fifty thousand" is off by 100. 1,496 (Ezra 8) as at B8.
- **E2/E3 named sources:**
  - **Reading and early learning:**
    - Bassok, Latham & Rorem 2016 ("Is Kindergarten the New First Grade?");
    - Yogman et al. 2018 (AAP, "The Power of Play");
    - Jane Healy, _Endangered Minds_;
    - Raymond Moore;
    - Suggate et al. 2013;
    - Joe Frost;
    - Michael Gurian;
    - Dee & Sievertsen 2018;
    - Harvard Center on the Developing Child, Working Paper 2;
    - Lego Foundation 2018;
    - Lisa Nielsen;
    - "Catherine Davies" (probably Catherine Davis).
  - **Myopia:** Ian Morgan / Dolgin, _Nature_ 2015; Young et al. 1969; Thorn et al. 2005.
  - **E3 additions:** Nicholas Kardaras, _Glow Kids_ (2016); Dan Greenberg; Gatto, _Dumbing Us Down_; the Animal School (George Reavis, fable).
  - **Unidentified:** "Allen Wolf", "Dr DeSarli", "300 late starters" (C4: 9,300), the "science vs reading" kindergarten, "30 hours" to learn to read.
  - **Wrong as stated:**
    - "14% better on IQ tests" (Schoenthaler 1986 measured achievement percentiles);
    - "over 90% of Seventh-day Adventists voted for Hitler" (no such data; the NSDAP peaked at 37.3% in July 1932 and 43.9% in March 1933; ~90% is the 1934 plebiscite);
    - the Counter-Reformation "began in Germany";
    - "80% of dropouts are boys" (US ≈ 55–60%).
- **F. C. Gilbert, _Ministry_ 1933** (E7): as at C13. Errors: "rabbi a degree of Greek origin"; "the Sanhedrin which crucified Jesus"; "gaon" anachronistic.

---

**Counts:** VERIFIED 68 · DIFFERS 1 · NOT FOUND 1

Per video (counted rows): F1 15 V · F2 5 V · F3 0 (one dup) · F4 25 V · F5 20 V, 1 D, 1 NF · F6 1 V · F7 2 V. F7 repeats almost all of F4's Ed chapter 31 readings (dups). AH 235.1 (F5 0:38:00) is the source behind C8 0:56:11's NOT FOUND "record your name beside David Livingstone, Hudson Taylor" row: an embellishment of "the recording angel writes her name as one of the greatest missionaries in the world".

### Non-EGW quotations

**F1 (A Thinking Generation)**

- Ray Kurzweil and Elon Musk on brain–computer links (opening hook, as C2); Carter G. Woodson, _The Mis-Education of the Negro_ ("if you can control a man's thinking…"); the Hitler line on youth (as C2; attribution weak). Leads only.
- Elisabeth Walling (the bell that ends the solar system for sentence dissection); Jane Healy's eight blocks (_Endangered Minds_ / _Your Child's Growing Mind_); Raymond Moore ("quietness, calmness and freedom from artificial excitement…", which he traces correctly to CG 139.1).
- David Elkind, _The Hurried Child_ (PhD from UCLA, not Tufts; he taught at Tufts); Doris Fromberg (Hofstra) on standardized tests. Checkable.
- "The New York study of 1 million students… 14% better on IQ tests": Schoenthaler et al. 1986 reported a rise in national achievement-test percentile rankings after additive/dye removal in NYC schools, not IQ.
- Peter Gray and Jay Feldman (age mixing; the one-room school); Daniel Greenberg (Sudbury Valley); Shyers (homeschool socialization, 1992 dissertation). Checkable.
- The Animal School (George Reavis, 1940s fable): told as a parable; drop its homeschool ending.
- Science numbers to drop or source: cerebellum "about 40% of neurons" (it holds ~80% of neurons); "thin and watery" semicircular-canal fluid in children; "only 5% of us actually think".

**F2 (Doing the Right Job at the Right Time)**

- Myopia: _Nature_ 2015 "The myopia boom" (Dolgin; retinal dopamine), Wu et al. 2013 Taiwan outdoor-recess trial (Ophthalmology), Ian Morgan (ANU) on outdoor light; Natal, Brazil prevalence figures (need a primary source). Checkable leads; his treatment advice (no glasses; no close work for months) is out.
- Jane Healy ("functional organization", "missing addend", inner speech); Raymond Moore (CNS immaturity and reading); Michael Gurian (boys' Ds and Fs; 80% of dropouts looks too high); _Early Childhood Research Quarterly_ (Suggate et al. 2013 on early vs late readers is the likely study); "a study of 300 individuals" (unknown; C4 said 9,300).
- NAAL 2003 (US adult literacy): the four bands are real, but "14% completely illiterate" is wrong (Below Basic ≠ illiterate); "almost 100%" in Scandinavia is a self-report figure.
- "Even the best daycare cannot completely neutralize…" (no source named); eidetic memory "most children" (a small minority in the studies).

**F3 / F6 (iChild)**

- William (4) and the iPad (Daily Mail, Jan 2014); Dr Richard Graham's technology-addiction programme (cost reported as £16,000, not "$20,000"); _The World Unplugged_ (University of Maryland, 2011, a qualitative 24-hour survey).
- Cris Rowan's ten reasons (Huffington Post, 2014); Common Sense Media 2008 review of 173 studies; Kaiser Family Foundation 2010 (_Generation M2_: supervision and bedroom media); Boston College 2012 (TIMSS/PIRLS sleep data); IARC Group 2B for RF fields (2011; correct but weak).
- Victoria Dunckley, "Dumb and Dumber: Interactive Screen Time Is Worse than TV" (Psychology Today) and her "electronic fast"; the Japanese 2,000-child sleep survey (needs a source); Dimitri Christakis (ages and AAP guidance); Loh & Kanai 2014 (media multitasking and the ACC); Nicholas Carr, _The Shallows_ (2010).
- Kyrene School District (NYT, Sept 2011; the $47 million proposal); Steve Jobs, Chris Anderson, Alan Eagle (NYT, Sept 2014 and Oct 2011); Paul Thomas (Furman); Waldorf "94% go on to college" (the school's own figure; "top universities/Ivy League" is wrong).
- To drop: "frontal lobe shuts off within 30 seconds"; the hypnosis story; left/right-brain imbalance from smartphones; blue light causing macular degeneration; "Dora Stromberg" TED talk (unidentified).

**F4 / F7 (Respectable Conventionality)**

- The Apple Pickers parable (unattributed; it circulates as "The Parable of the Apple Pickers"); E. M. Bounds, _Power Through Prayer_ ("Men are God's method…"; read correctly); John Wesley's "Give me one hundred men…" (a paraphrase of his 1777 letter to Alexander Mather; "in one generation" is a later addition).
- World-misery statistics: 19,000 under-5 deaths a day in 2011 (UNICEF, correct); ~30 million enslaved (Global Slavery Index 2013); 1 in 9 hungry (FAO 2014–16); "20 million orphans" and "22 million child refugees" need sources (UNICEF counts ~15 million double orphans).
- Edison's "10,000 tries" (folklore); Livingstone buried in Westminster Abbey (correct); Luther and Melanchthon "one generation, about 40 years" (simplified).
- The Kenyan orphanage director (secondhand; 253 children in F4, 257 in F7, 283 in D3).

**F5 (Will It Ever Be Easy)**

- George Barna, _Revolutionary Parenting_ (2007): parenting as primary job; counter-cultural cost. Checkable.
- Brooks-Gunn, Han & Waldfogel 2002 (_Child Development_; maternal employment by nine months and school readiness at 36 months; modest, group-dependent effects); Zhang et al. 2002 (infant rat separation and apoptosis) and monkey separation studies (animal work; leave out); Kochanska on early conscience. Checkable.
- "50% of marriages end in divorce" (old, overstated); "75% of our young people leaving the church" (no source); "Give me a child until he is seven…" (traditional Jesuit maxim, no primary source).
- Luther's "Art thou alone wise?" and "side by side with me is the Word of God": via GC 143.1 (Martyn) and GC 142.3 (D'Aubigné); read as Luther, not EGW.
